Regions Of Interest (ROI)

Running median is the median computed along the genome using a sliding window. The following tables give regions of interest detected by sequana. Here are some definition of the table's columns:

  • mean_cov: the average of coverage
  • mean_rm: the average of running median
  • mean_zscore: the average of zscore
  • max_zscore: the higher zscore contains in the region
  • log2_ratio:log2(mean_cov/mean_rm)

Low coverage region

Regions with a z-score lower than -2.00 and at least one base with a z-score lower than -4.00 are detected as low coverage region. Thus, there are 0 low coverage regions between the position 1 and the position 12729

chr,start,end,size,mean_cov,max_cov,mean_rm,mean_zscore,max_zscore,log2_ratio
chr start end size mean_cov max_cov mean_rm mean_zscore max_zscore log2_ratio

High coverage region

Regions with a z-score higher than 2.00 and at least one base with a z-score higher than 4.00 are detected as high coverage region. Thus, there are 0 high coverage regions between the position 1 and the position 12729

chr,start,end,size,mean_cov,max_cov,mean_rm,mean_zscore,max_zscore,log2_ratio
chr start end size mean_cov max_cov mean_rm mean_zscore max_zscore log2_ratio
